READII-2-ROQC provides a scalable quality-control strategy for developing interpretable, biologically grounded imaging biomarkers and reproducible radiomics workflows.

</details>


### [88] [ViewMind3D: Modular View-Aware Inference for Training-Free 3D-QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28442)
*Ping-Kun Chiang,Kun-Ru Wu,Po-han Li,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViewMind3D是一个完全免训练的模块化3D问答框架，通过将任务分解为多视角选择、视觉定位、BEV空间编码和角色推理四个可解释组件，利用通用LLM和VLM实现3D空间推理，在ScanQA和SQA3D基准上达到与微调3D-LLM相竞争的性能（SQA3D准确率50.8%，ScanQA CIDEr 73.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D问答方法大多依赖3D特定训练或微调，需要大量昂贵的标注数据，限制了可扩展性和真实世界适用性。作者旨在探索一种无需训练、无需完整3D重建的模块化3D推理框架。

Method: 将3D-QA任务分解为四个可解释组件：(1) 问题驱动的多视角选择；(2) 利用语言条件对象线索进行引导式视觉定位；(3) 通过鸟瞰图(BEV)视点指示器编码空间上下文；(4) 通过基于角色的推理生成结构化答案。整个框架通过编排通用LLM和VLM实现，无需任何模型微调。

Result: 在ScanQA上达到73.4 CIDEr，在SQA3D上整体准确率达50.8%，与先前免训练和微调的3D-LLM方法相比具有竞争力，尤其在SQA3D的"What"类空间接地问题上表现提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过通用LLM和VLM的模块化编排，可以在不进行3D特定训练的情况下实现有效的3D空间推理，为机器人感知和具身AI提供了一种可扩展、可解释且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled new possibilities for 3D question answering (3D-QA), a key capability for embodied AI and robotic perception. However, most existing methods rely on 3D-specific training or fine-tuning with costly annotations, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present \textbf{ViewMind3D}, a fully training-free and modular framework for 3D spatial reasoning over multi-view observations of a scene without requiring complete 3D reconstruction. The framework decomposes the 3D-QA task into four interpretable components: (1) question-driven multi-view selection, (2) guided visual grounding with language-conditioned object cues, (3) spatial context encoding via a bird's-eye-view (BEV) viewpoint indicator, and (4) structured answer generation through role-based reasoning. This design enables structured, robust, and interpretable reasoning without requiring model tuning. Experimental results on ScanQA and SQA3D show that ViewMind3D achieves competitive performance compared to prior training-free and fine-tuned 3D-LLMs. In particular, our method improves performance on spatially grounded question types, such as ``What'' questions in SQA3D, while maintaining strong overall accuracy (50.8\%) and achieving 73.4 CIDEr on ScanQA. These results demonstrate that effective 3D reasoning can be achieved through modular orchestration of general-purpose LLMs and VLMs for robotic perception in real-world environments.

</details>


### [89] [VisualRouter: Query-Grounded Visual Sampling for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28463)
*Haiyue Zhang,Yi Bin,Xun Jiang,Zeyu Ma,Duo Peng,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisualRouter，一个无需训练、即插即用的基于查询的视觉采样框架，通过将查询分类为全局或局部并应用相应采样策略，在有限输入帧下联合平衡相关性、覆盖度和多样性，在多个长视频理解基准上稳定提升多种LVLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临视觉token数量大和上下文窗口有限的挑战。现有视觉采样方法存在不足：相关感知采样会导致帧选择冗余、时间覆盖不足；固定采样策略忽略了查询类型差异。需要一种能根据查询自适应采样的方法。

Method: VisualRouter首先将每个查询分类为全局或局部两类。对全局查询，采用相关性-覆盖度混合策略，在保留时间覆盖度的同时保留与查询相关的视觉证据；对局部查询，采用事件感知帧选择策略，进行事件划分、片段级帧分配和事件内帧选择，在有限帧数下联合平衡相关性、覆盖度和多样性。该方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU三个基准上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B分别获得5.2%、7.7%和11.6%的提升，持续优于均匀采样，且在相同设置下超越现有无需训练的视觉采样方法。

Conclusion: VisualRouter通过查询驱动的自适应采样策略有效解决了长视频理解中视觉采样的核心矛盾，证明了将查询类型感知与事件结构相结合的采样思路对提升多种LVLM长视频理解能力的普适有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved significant progress in video understanding, yet understanding long videos remains challenging due to the large number of visual tokens and limited context windows. Visual sampling provides a practical solution by selecting an informative subset of frames. However, existing methods typically either rely on relevance-aware sampling, leading to redundant frame selection and insufficient temporal coverage, or adopt a fixed sampling strategy regardless of query type. In this paper, we propose VisualRouter, a training-free and plug-and-play framework for query-grounded visual sampling. VisualRouter first classifies each query as either global or local and then applies the corresponding sampling strategy. For global queries, it employs a relevance-coverage hybrid strategy that preserves temporal coverage while retaining query-relevant visual evidence. For local queries, it adopts an event-aware frame selection strategy that performs event partitioning, segment-level frame allocation, and intra-event frame selection, jointly balancing relevance, coverage, and diversity with a limited number of input frames. Experiments show that VisualRouter consistently improves multiple LVLMs over uniform sampling, achieving gains of 5.2%, 7.7%, and 11.6% on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and outperforming existing training-free visual sampling methods under the same setting.

</details>


### [90] [Can Vision-Language Models Reason about AI Edits in Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28464)
*Darsha Udayanga,Pin-Yu Chen,Payel Das,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GRPO（组相对策略优化）的强化学习框架，训练视觉语言模型（VLMs）对AI篡改图像进行推理检测和定位。该方法通过简单准确率与格式奖励激励模型生成结构化思考轨迹，再引导轻量级分割模型生成像素级定位掩码。实验在多个图像篡改数据集上取得了与最先进伪造检测器相当的性能，并引入统一的eff-IoU指标同时评估检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二元分类器检测图像篡改缺乏可解释性和泛化能力；现有的VLM方法依赖带有监督的微调和精选解释，未能充分利用VLM固有的推理能力。因此需要探索一种无需显式推理监督的方法，利用强化学习激活VLM对AI生成内容的内在推理能力。

Method: 提出基于GRPO的强化学习训练框架：1) 采用简单准确率奖励和格式奖励而非人工标注的解释监督；2) 模型接收输入图像后生成结构化思考轨迹，并输出篡改判断；3) 推理输出用于引导轻量级分割模型生成像素级定位掩码；4) 引入eff-IoU统一指标同时评估检测准确性和定位质量。

Result: 在多个图像篡改数据集上，该方法在检测和定位性能上与最先进的图像伪造检测器相媲美，且所需监督显著更弱（仅需简单的准确率与格式信号，无需精选解释标注）。

Conclusion: 强化学习为训练VLM推理AI生成内容提供了一种有效且可扩展的机制，证明了无需显式推理监督即可激活模型的推理能力，为AI篡改检测提供了一种更轻量、可解释且具泛化能力的新范式。

Abstract: Detection and localization of AI-tampered images are critical for trustworthy AI, yet modern generative models have made such manipulations increasingly difficult to identify. While traditional binary classifiers can detect image tampering, they lack interpretability and generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative due to their strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities; however, existing approaches typically rely on supervised finetuning with curated explanations rather than exploiting their inherent reasoning capabilities. In this work, we investigate whether VLMs can be trained to reason about AI-generated image edits using reinforcement learning (RL) rather than explicit reasoning supervision. Motivated by the success in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an RL technique that incentivizes the model to reason by generating thinking traces prior to giving the final answer, we propose a GRPO-based training framework that utilizes simple accuracy and format rewards. Given an input image, the model produces a structured reasoning trace and predicts whether the image has been tampered with. A lightweight segmentation model is then guided by the reasoning output to generate pixel-level localization masks. Experiments across multiple image manipulation datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive detection and localization performance compared to state-of-the-art image forgery detectors, despite requiring substantially weaker supervision. We introduce effective intersection over union (eff-IoU), a unified metric to jointly evaluate detection and localization. These results suggest that reinforcement learning provides an effective and scalable mechanism for teaching VLMs to reason about AI-generated content.

</details>


### [91] [Towards Real-Time PixOOD: Efficient Anomaly Segmentation for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28483)
*Luca de Martino,Federico Aromolo,Federico Nesti,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的实时异常分割加速流水线，通过重构PixOOD的Neyman-Pearson评分阶段并结合TensorRT硬件优化编译，在桌面级NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU上达到182 FPS（提速20倍），在嵌入式Jetson AGX Orin平台上达到75 FPS（提速18倍），实现了自动驾驶和铁路场景中异常分割的实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时异常分割方法虽然精度较高，但计算开销过大，难以在嵌入式硬件上部署，限制了其在自动驾驶和铁路等安全关键系统中的实际应用。

Method: 对最先进的分布外检测方法PixOOD的Neyman-Pearson评分阶段进行重构优化，并将完整流水线通过硬件优化的TensorRT编译进行部署加速，同时面向嵌入式与桌面端平台设计。

Result: 在桌面NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU上达到182 FPS，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式平台上达到75 FPS，分别比原始基线快20倍和18倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，先进的异常分割技术可以有效部署于自动驾驶和铁路应用的车载实时处理中，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Real-time anomaly segmentation is essential for the safety of autonomous systems. Although recent approaches offer high accuracy, their computational cost limits their deployment on embedded hardware. This work presents an efficient and accelerated pipeline designed for both embedded and desktop platforms, targeting the autonomous driving and railway domains. The proposed approach reformulates the Neyman-Pearson scoring stage of PixOOD, a state-of-the-art out-of-distribution detection method, and deploys the full pipeline through hardware-optimized TensorRT compilation, reaching up to 182 FPS on a desktop NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU and 75 FPS on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin embedded platform, respectively 20x and 18x faster than the original baseline. The achieved results demonstrate that advanced anomaly segmentation can be efficiently deployed for onboard processing in autonomous driving and railway applications.

</details>


### [92] [AuricularWorld: Hierarchical Action-Guided World Modeling for Fine-Grained Auricular Structure Segmentation from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28487)
*Jingwen Yang,Senmao Wang,Luoyao Kang,Runmeng Cui,Keying Zhang,Yunjia Bao,Haifan Gong,Lin Lin,Haiyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于世界模型的CT耳廓结构精细分割框架，通过在编码器-解码器架构的中间隐空间中引入确定性循环状态空间模型，实现迭代解剖推理。该框架在推理时执行三步潜在展开，通过分层解剖动作逐步细化隐表示，在CT耳廓小、不规则且重叠的结构上分割精度显著提升，HD95降低超过43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT中耳廓结构精细分割极具挑战性：耳部占据图像区域小、软骨边界高度不规则、软骨与周围软组织界面模糊；同时临床标注可能同时包含复合结构（软骨+相邻皮肤）和对应的纯软骨区域，产生嵌套重叠标签。传统前馈预测方法难以有效处理这些复杂解剖结构。

Method: 基于编码器-解码器架构，在中间隐空间引入确定性循环状态空间模型。多尺度编码器特征与部分解码表示融合形成结构观测来初始化隐动力学。推理时执行三步潜在展开（无需真实标签引导），分层解剖动作更新循环状态并逐步细化隐表示，最终投影回解码器并与高分辨率特征结合生成分割结果。训练中引入平衡分层动作目标，处理前景稀疏、解剖组缺失以及加/减操作不平衡问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提框架在CT中体积小、形状不规则且相互重叠的耳廓结构分割上持续提升准确率，HD95降低超过43%。

Conclusion: 隐空间世界模型推理对于具有挑战性的医学图像分割任务具有显著有效性，为超越传统前馈预测的迭代解剖推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of auricular structures in CT is challenging because the ear occupies a small image region, cartilage boundaries are highly irregular, and interfaces between cartilage and surrounding soft tissues are often ambiguous. Clinical annotations may also include both composite structures containing cartilage and adjacent skin and their corresponding cartilage-only regions, producing nested and overlapping labels. We propose a world-model-based segmentation framework that enables iterative anatomical reasoning beyond conventional feed-forward prediction. Built on an encoder-decoder architecture, the framework introduces a deterministic recurrent state-space model into the intermediate latent space. Multi-scale encoder features and partially decoded representations are fused to form a structural observation that initializes the latent dynamics. During inference, the model performs a three-step latent rollout without ground-truth guidance. Hierarchical anatomical actions update the recurrent state and progressively refine the latent representation. The resulting latent trajectory is projected back into the decoder and combined with high-resolution features to produce the final segmentation. To learn reliable latent transitions, we introduce a balanced hierarchical action objective that addresses foreground sparsity, missing anatomical groups, and imbalance between add and remove operations. Extensive experiments show that the proposed framework consistently improves segmentation accuracy and reduces HD95 by more than 43% for small, irregular, and overlapping auricular structures in CT. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of latent world-model reasoning for challenging medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [93] [RefCaptioner: Multi-Reference Image-Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28509)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Yuqi Tang,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Weihong Lin,Xinlei Yu,Yujie Wei,Xinwei Long,Fengxiang Wang,Xinlong Chen,Yue Ding,Jialu Chen,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多参考图像视频字幕生成任务，并设计了RefCaptioner两阶段后训练框架，通过混合数据SFT和分级覆盖率折扣GRPO提升参考选择、短语级绑定、干扰项拒绝和跨参考一致性，在MRVBench基准上达到开源模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频字幕生成模型虽然能生成自然语言描述，但无法将局部视觉元素显式地关联到多个参考图像上。这一局限性使得模型缺乏细粒度的参考定位能力，难以满足需要跨模态事实性验证的下游应用需求。

Method: 提出了两阶段后训练框架RefCaptioner：(1)混合数据SFT用于初步学习参考选择与短语级绑定；(2)Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO（分级覆盖率折扣的分组相对策略优化）用于联合优化参考选择、短语级绑定、干扰项拒绝和跨参考一致性，同时保持通用视频字幕生成能力。构建了包含20,000个视频和171,354张参考图像的训练语料，并设计了MRVBench评测基准。

Result: 在MRVBench上，RefCaptioner在开源模型中取得最优综合性能，同时在标准视频字幕基准上保持竞争力。人工评估表明其字幕更受标注者偏好，并能实现更忠实于源视频的内容重建（无论使用开源还是专有视频生成器）。

Conclusion: RefCaptioner有效解决了多参考图像引导的视频字幕生成这一新任务，验证了将强化学习与混合数据微调相结合的后训练策略在细粒度跨模态对齐全景下的可靠性与通用性。

Abstract: Existing video captioning models generate natural descriptions of video content but cannot explicitly ground local visual elements to multiple reference images. We introduce multi-reference image-grounded video captioning, a new task requiring factual video descriptions with phrase-level reference grounding, and propose RefCaptioner, a two-stage post-training framework for this task. RefCaptioner combines mixed-data SFT with Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO to jointly improve reference selection, phrase-level binding, distractor rejection, and cross-reference consistency while preserving general video-captioning ability. To support training, we construct a corpus containing $20,000$ videos and 171,354 reference images. We further introduce MRVBench, a benchmark for evaluating caption factuality and multi-reference grounding on both real-world and AI-generated videos. Experiments show that RefCaptioner achieves the best overall performance among the open-source models while remaining competitive on standard video captioning benchmarks. Human evaluation further confirms that its captions are preferred by annotators and enable more source-faithful video reconstruction with both open-source and proprietary video generators.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Frame Selection: Generative Latent Evidence Aggregation for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28516)
*Bowen Liu,Shuning Wang,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenEvA是一个生成式潜在证据聚合框架，通过在视频帧选择之后增加一个查询相关的跨帧证据整合阶段，将选中的帧组织成语义互补的潜在证据，从而提升长视频理解中视频-多模态大语言模型的问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视频理解压缩管线（如帧或视觉token选择）只关注保留相关的显式视觉内容，但仅仅提供证据并不能确保跨时刻的互补线索被整合用于答案生成。作者提出关键假设：在帧选择之后，需要将选中的帧组织成查询相关的跨帧证据，才能真正提升回答质量。

Method: 将帧选择后的阶段形式化为一个潜在证据接口（latent evidence interface），并实例化为GenEvA框架。GenEvA使用查询条件化的证据分布（query-conditioned evidence distribution）来聚焦帧相关的聚合，从帧特化信息中形成紧凑的跨帧潜在证据。同时，由于跨帧整合并非总是必要，同一分布还决定是否插入这一潜在补充（即自适应证据调用，Adaptive Evidence Invocation）。

Result: 在四个基准和两个Video-MLLM骨干上，GenEvA一致优于匹配帧的基线。在8帧设置下，LLaVA-Video在四个基准上的平均性能提升+5.2点，Qwen2.5-VL在LVBench上的准确率提升+10.1点。这些提升仅需平均0.11%-0.40%的视频token开销。分析进一步显示了任务感知的分配以及自适应证据调用的收益。

Conclusion: 在帧选择之后引入查询相关的跨帧潜在证据聚合阶段，能够以极低的token开销显著提升长视频理解性能，验证了"证据可用不等于证据被整合"这一核心观点，并展示了自适应跨帧整合的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Long-video understanding commonly compresses videos into a small set of frames or visual tokens for answer generation. Existing compact pipelines focus on retaining relevant visual content as explicit evidence. Yet making evidence available does not ensure that complementary cues across moments are integrated for answering. Our key idea is to organize selected frames into query-relevant cross-frame evidence before generation. We formulate this post-selection stage as a latent evidence interface and instantiate it with GenEvA ($\textbf{Gen}erative$ $Latent$ $\textbf{Ev}idence$ $\textbf{A}ggregation$), a distribution-guided latent evidence aggregation framework. Specifically, GenEvA uses a query-conditioned evidence distribution to focus aggregation on relevant frames, forming compact cross-frame latent evidence from their frame-specific information. Since cross-frame integration is not always needed, the same distribution determines whether to insert this latent complement. Across four benchmarks and two Video-MLLM backbones, GenEvA consistently improves matched-frame baselines. At 8 frames, it raises the four-benchmark LLaVA-Video average by $+5.2$ points and Qwen2.5-VL accuracy on LVBench by $+10.1$ points. These gains require only $0.11\%$--$0.40\%$ average video-token overhead; analyses further show task-aware allocation and benefits from Adaptive Evidence Invocation.

</details>


### [95] [What to Remove, What to Preserve: Dual-Ambiguity Rectification for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28526)
*Cencen Liu,Wen Yin,Dongyang Zhang,Dongmin Li,Shan Zhao,Bing Su,Tao He,Jielei Wang,Guoming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAR-Net，一个用于全合一图像复原的双重模糊校正网络。它通过退化原型表示（DAR）模块构建结构化退化状态，并通过语义模糊校正（SeAR）和空间模糊校正（SpAR）模块分别解决通道调制中的语义模糊和复原响应中的空间模糊问题，在多种退化复原基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法通常在共享潜在空间中编码异质退化条件，导致退化相关线索与场景内容相互纠缠。作者将其表征为双重模糊问题：通道维度调制的语义模糊和复原响应的空间模糊，这些问题会导致内容损坏和残余伪影，亟需针对性解决。

Method: DAR-Net包含三个核心模块：1）退化原型表示（DAR）模块，通过单纯形约束的原型混合建模构建结构化退化状态；2）语义模糊校正（SeAR）模块，基于退化状态生成退化感知提示以改善解码器的通道级条件化；3）空间模糊校正（SpAR）模块，将退化感知特征与互补特征正则化到正交响应子空间中，减少去除与保留线索之间的空间干扰。

Result: 在标准全合一复原基准上，DAR-Net在三种退化和五种退化设置下均取得最佳综合性能；平均PSNR分别比最强竞争者提升0.14 dB（三退化设置）和0.34 dB（五退化设置）；在CDD-11和WeatherBench上也表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: DAR-Net通过双重模糊校正策略有效解决了全合一图像复原中的退化耦合问题，在多个基准上验证了方法的有效性，为全合一复原任务提供了新的思路。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified framework. Existing methods commonly encode heterogeneous degradation conditions in a shared latent space, where degradation-related cues and scene content can remain entangled. We characterize the resulting challenge as dual ambiguity: semantic ambiguity in channel-wise modulation and spatial ambiguity in restoration responses, which can lead to content corruption and residual artifacts. To mitigate this issue, we propose DAR-Net, a Dual-Ambiguity Rectification Network for all-in-one image restoration. DAR-Net first introduces a Degradation Archetype Representation (DAR) module to construct a structured degradation state through simplex-constrained archetype mixture modeling. Based on this state, a Semantic Ambiguity Rectification (SeAR) module generates degradation-aware prompts to improve channel-wise conditioning in the decoder. A Spatial Ambiguity Rectification (SpAR) module further regularizes degradation-aware and complementary features toward orthogonal response subspaces, reducing spatial interference between removal and preservation cues. Extensive experiments on standard all-in-one restoration benchmarks show that DAR-Net achieves the best overall performance under both three-degradation and five-degradation settings, improving the average PSNR over the strongest competitor by 0.14 dB and 0.34 dB, respectively; it additionally shows superior performance on CDD-11 and WeatherBench.

</details>


### [96] [MarkushGlyph and OCSRGlyph: Improved Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28532)
*Alex Andonian,Samuel G Rodriques,Andrew D White,Siddharth M Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCSRGlyph和MarkushGlyph两个模型，分别用于光学化学结构识别（OCSR）和Markush结构解析，将两者统一为图像到文本的翻译任务，并引入新的Markush结构翻译评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学结构在专利和科学文献中以图像形式出现，需要转换为行符号表示以便程序化使用。虽然单分子图像识别（OCSR）相对成熟，但Markush结构（表示分子家族）的解析仍然具有挑战性，且现有评估指标存在缺陷。

Method: 将OCSR和Markush结构解析统一视为图像到文本的翻译问题。提出OCSRGlyph，通过仔细考虑立体化学来提高单分子识别性能；提出MarkushGlyph，一种视觉-语言模型，将整个Markush结构作为图像读取，与先前多阶段分别处理视觉和文本输入的系统形成对比。此外，引入新的Markush结构翻译准确率评估指标。

Result: OCSRGlyph在OCSR任务中达到最先进性能，超越了先前方法；MarkushGlyph能够直接从图像端到端读取完整Markush结构；新提出的评估指标解决了先前指标中存在的失败模式。

Conclusion: 将两种化学结构识别任务都建模为图像到文本翻译可以获得优异效果，特别是MarkushGlyph的端到端视觉-语言方法相比多阶段系统更具优势，同时新的评估指标更加可靠地衡量Markush结构翻译准确性。

Abstract: Chemical structures appear in patents and the scientific literature as images. For programmatic usage, such as indexing in databases or constructing machine learning model training sets, they must be transformed into line notations. The two common forms of this task are translating an image of a single molecule (optical chemical structure recognition - OCSR) and translating a Markush structure that represents a family of molecules. While prior work in the former case is quite mature, Markush structure parsing remains a challenging task. In this work, we treat both tasks as an image-to-text translation problem. We then propose OCSRGlyph, a state-of-the-art OCSR model, improving performance over prior methods by carefully considering stereochemistry. For the Markush task, we introduce MarkushGlyph, a vision-language model that reads the entire Markush structure as an image. This contrasts with prior systems, which often use multiple stages to separately process visual and text input content. Finally, we introduce a new metric for determining the accuracy of Markush structure translations, handling failure modes present in prior metrics.

</details>


### [97] [ScaFE: Data-Efficient Scar Classification with LLM-Generated Clinical Feature Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28538)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaFE框架，利用LLM生成可执行的特征程序进行病理瘢痕分类，在本地环境执行并返回聚合统计信息，兼顾数据治理和可审计性，在跨医院数据上超越基线方法10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理性瘢痕（瘢痕疙瘩与增生性瘢痕）的临床照片分类面临专家标注数据稀缺和跨医院采集差异大的挑战。端到端图像模型数据依赖性强，而将照片发送给云端视觉语言模型（VLM）可能违反本地数据治理要求，且决策难以复现和审计。

Method: ScaFE（Scar Feature Engineering）通过以下步骤实现：1）利用支持联网的LLM检索临床证据并合成测量可视瘢痕属性的特征程序；2）候选程序在受限本地环境中执行，仅返回聚合验证统计量和特征级SHAP摘要用于迭代修复；3）原始图像和患者级输出保持本地；4）轻量级随机森林在结构化特征表示上进行分类。

Result: 在三家医院600张照片的leave-one-site-out评估中，ScaFE达到81.0%的站点宏平衡准确率，超过最强基线BiomedCLIP 10个百分点；仅用10%开发数据时仍保持72.0%平衡准确率且有11.8个百分点的领先。迭代精修将可执行程序率从66.7%提升至95.0%，91.7%的最终特征有已验证证据。

Conclusion: LLM知识可以通过本地、可审计的特征程序而非直接的VLM决策，来支持数据高效、跨站点的医学图像分类任务。

Abstract: Classifying pathological scars from clinical photographs requires distinguishing keloids from hypertrophic scars despite limited expert-labeled data and substantial acquisition variation across hospitals. End-to-end image models remain data-dependent, whereas sending photographs to a hosted vision-language model (VLM) may conflict with local data-governance requirements and yields decisions that are difficult to reproduce and audit. We introduce ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering), which transfers clinical knowledge from a large language model (LLM) into deterministic, executable feature programs instead of asking the model to diagnose images. A web-enabled LLM retrieves clinical evidence and synthesizes programs that measure visually assessable scar attributes. Candidate programs execute in a restricted local environment, and only aggregate validation statistics and feature-level SHAP summaries are returned for iterative repair and refinement; raw images and patient-level outputs remain local. A lightweight Random Forest then operates on the resulting structured representation. On 600 photographs from three hospitals under leave-one-site-out evaluation, ScaFE achieves 81.0% site-macro balanced accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline, BiomedCLIP, by 10.0 percentage points. With only 10% of the development data, ScaFE retains 72.0% balanced accuracy and an 11.8-point lead. Iterative refinement also raises the executable-program rate from 66.7% to 95.0%, with verified evidence for 91.7% of the final features. These results show that LLM knowledge can support data-efficient, cross-site medical image classification through local and auditable feature programs rather than direct VLM decisions.

</details>


### [98] [MIND: Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers for Medical Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28565)
*Yunzhan Fu,Xiangyu Shen,Yifei Sun,Yuhan Chen,Jian Wu,Hongxia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIND网络，利用BioMedGPT生成意图驱动文本引导医学图像融合，通过DiTs架构、多尺度潜在适配器和医学语义一致性损失，在哈佛、BraTS和GFP数据集上实现更优融合质量并提升下游脑肿瘤分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像融合方法使用全局统一的融合规则，缺乏对诊断意图和病理结构的深入理解，难以满足临床诊断对病理性知识引导的需求。

Method: 1) 利用BioMedGPT从源图像生成意图驱动的融合文本，提供病理感知的诊断意图引导；2) 设计多尺度潜在适配器（Multi-scale Latent Adapter），在序列化前显式提取源图像特征，通过严格维度对齐注入网络，缓解DiTs中1D序列展平导致的2D空间连续性损失；3) 设计医学语义一致性损失，确保融合图像与融合文本之间的深层语义锁定，同时维持底层物理流形重构的稳定性。

Result: 在哈佛、BraTS和GFP数据集上的综合实验表明：MIND获得更优的融合质量，显著提升下游脑肿瘤分割精度，并支持灵活的交互式融合。

Conclusion: MIND证明了意图驱动的扩散Transformer架构在医学图像融合中的有效性，为智能临床决策支持系统提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Medical image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from diverse imaging modalities to support clinical diagnosis. Existing methods typically apply uniform fusion rules globally, lacking a deep understanding of diagnostic intents and pathological structures. To address these limitations, we propose MIND, a Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for medical image fusion. Specifically, we utilize BioMedGPT to generate intent-driven fusion texts from source images, guiding the fusion process with pathology-aware diagnostic intents. To combat the loss of 2D spatial continuity caused by 1D sequence flattening in DiTs, we design a Multi-scale Latent Adapter. This module explicitly extracts source image features before serialization, injecting them into the network via strict dimensional alignment to effectively supplement image features. To resolve the semantic shift caused by decoupling image outputs from diagnostic intents, we design a medical semantic consistency loss. This loss ensures deep semantic locking between fused images and fusion texts while maintaining the stability of the underlying physical manifold reconstruction. Comprehensive experiments on the Harvard, BraTS, and GFP datasets reveal that MIND delivers superior fusion quality, significantly improves downstream brain tumor segmentation accuracy, and enables flexible interactive fusion, holding significant promise for intent-driven intelligent clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [99] [Finding Change in Satellite Archives from Text: How to Combine Before-and-After Images Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28571)
*Simon Roy,Mark Bong,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性对比了八种双时相卫星图像变化描述检索的融合模块设计，涵盖注意力、状态空间模型（Mamba）和学习的压缩（Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF）三大类别。研究发现：（1）无训练的二级搜索（廉价差分模型粗筛+注意力融合精排）可在保持召回率的同时将查询成本降低10-15倍；（2）Mamba的线性扫描在视觉Transformer典型patch数（L=196）下受内存带宽限制，无速度优势，而注意力能更好映射到并行硬件；（3）TBF可将参数减少2.3倍、延迟降低1.6倍，仅牺牲0.007的BLEU-1，但更激进的压缩会悄悄丢弃对变化重要的细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务地球观测需要在大规模双时相卫星图像档案中执行自然语言变化描述检索（如"查找出现新建筑的图像对"）。融合模块（结合前后视图的组件）必须在查询时对所有候选对运行，其速度在很大程度上决定了每次搜索的代价，因此亟需系统性地比较和优化该模块的设计。

Method: 在固定一个图像编码器（冻结的CLIP模型）和一个训练方案的前提下，评估来自三大类别的八种融合模块设计：注意力机制、状态空间模型（Mamba）、学习压缩（提出的Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF）。每个设计在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC两个基准上使用十个随机种子进行测试，确保差异具有统计显著性。

Result: （1）训练免的二级搜索方法在LEVIR-CC上与全融合的召回率相当或更好，同时将查询成本降低10-15倍，在Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5表现相当；（2）Mamba在L=196的patch数下因内存带宽限制没有获得速度收益；（3）TBF将参数减少2.3倍、延迟降低1.6倍，仅牺牲0.007的BLEU-1，但过度压缩会丢失变化相关的细节。

Conclusion: 融合模块的架构选择应基于实际硬件和部署场景，而非理论上的复杂度优势；训练免的粗排-精排两级搜索策略是降低查询成本的有效途径；在压缩融合表示时需要警惕聚合指标掩盖的细节丢失问题。

Abstract: Operational Earth observation increasingly calls for answering queries such as ``find the image pairs where a new building appeared.'' This means searching an archive of before-and-after (bi-temporal) satellite image pairs and ranking each pair by how well it matches a natural-language description of the change. The component that performs this match, the fusion module that combines the ``before'' and ``after'' views, must be run at query time across many candidate pairs, so its speed largely sets the cost of every search. We present a controlled comparison of how to build that module. Using one fixed image encoder (a frozen CLIP model) and one training recipe for all variants, we evaluate eight designs drawn from three families: attention, state-space models (Mamba), and learned compression (our Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF). Each design is tested on two benchmarks (LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC) with ten random seeds, so the reported differences are statistically grounded. We outline three findings: first, a training-free two-stage search (a cheap difference model that shortlists candidates, followed by attention fusion that re-ranks them) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost $10$-$15\times$, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; second, the linear-time scan of Mamba, attractive on paper, gives no speed benefit at the patch counts typical of vision transformers ($L{=}196$): the scan is limited by memory bandwidth, whereas attention maps cleanly onto parallel hardware; and third, compressing the fused representation (TBF) reduces parameters by $2.3\times$ and latency by $1.6\times$ for a change-only BLEU-1 cost of $0.007$, although more aggressive compression quietly discards change-relevant detail that aggregate metrics fail to reveal.

</details>


### [100] [ROAD: Reciprocal-Objective Alignment of Discriminative Semantics for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28581)
*Xiao Luo,Mingyang Du,Xin Zhou,Tianrui Feng,Xiwu Chen,Xiaofan Li,Jiangning Zhang,Dingkang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROAD框架通过将判别式3D基础模型中的丰富先验知识迁移到扩散Transformer中，以极低的训练成本（仅1.5%的训练数据）实现高保真3D生成，达到与工业级基线Step1X-3D相竞争的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D生成传统上依赖扩大模型容量和数据规模，计算成本极高，且通常从零学习几何，忽略了判别式3D基础模型中已封装的丰富语义和结构先验。作者认为利用这些判别模型的3D世界理解能力可显著降低生成成本。

Method: 提出ROAD框架，将判别式3D基础模型的先验迁移到扩散Transformer中。核心是引入互惠目标对齐策略（reciprocal-objective alignment），包含两部分：1) 整体语义凝聚（Holistic Semantic Condensing）以强制全局语义一致性；2) 结构最优对齐（Structural Optimal Alignment），将微观几何细节对齐形式化为二部图匹配问题。3D基础模型仅用于训练时对齐监督，推理时不使用，不增加额外推理开销。

Result: 与工业基线Step1X-3D相比，ROAD仅使用1.5%的训练数据即实现极具竞争力的生成性能，显著降低了高保真3D生成的训练成本和计算开销。

Conclusion: 利用判别式3D基础模型中已有的先验知识而不是从零学习几何，可以有效降低3D生成的训练成本，同时保持高保真质量。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generation predominantly relies on scaling model capacity and data, which incurs prohibitive computational costs. This paradigm typically requires learning geometry from scratch and overlooks the rich semantic and structural priors already encapsulated in discriminative 3D foundation models. We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/ROAD.

</details>


### [101] [MixFrag: Fragility-Guided Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28589)
*Md. Mehrab Hossain Opi,Robiul Islam Ryad,Md. Umar Faruk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixFrag是一个基于脆弱性引导的混合精度后训练量化框架，通过KL散度估计组件级量化脆弱性，并将位宽分配建模为多选背包问题（MCKP），在目标位预算下实现自适应的逐层精度分配。在ImageNet-1K分类、COCO目标检测和实例分割任务上均取得competitive或state-of-the-art性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法对Vision Transformer的各个组件采用统一的位宽，忽略了不同组件对量化的异构敏感性（heterogeneous sensitivity），导致精度分配效率低下。因此需要一种能够感知组件脆弱性并自适应分配精度的混合精度PTQ方法。

Method: MixFrag包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过测量全精度与单独量化输出分布之间的KL散度来估计组件级量化脆弱性，仅需少量校准数据；(2) 将位宽分配问题形式化为多选背包问题（MCKP），在目标位预算约束下实现自适应的逐层精度分配。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上多个ViT架构上取得了competitive的分类性能；在COCO目标检测和实例分割任务上，MixFrag在挑战性的MP3/MP3设置下比之前最佳方法提升了最多9.6 AP，达到state-of-the-art性能。额外分析验证了脆弱性度量与学习到的位宽分配之间具有很强的相关性。

Conclusion: MixFrag通过脆弱性引导的混合精度分配有效解决了ViT中不同组件量化敏感性差异的问题，被验证为Vision Transformers混合精度后训练量化的有效框架。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as an effective solution for deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [102] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang,Yixing Li,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jianghao Lin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD），一种反事实目标重构算法，用于从教师修正中估计视觉可归因部分，在多模态对策略蒸馏中避免源混合监督问题，在六个细粒度视觉基准上优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态在线对策略蒸馏（OPD）虽然通过特权视图教师监督学生生成的轨迹来迁移细粒度视觉知识，但其下一词修正存在源混合问题——将视觉信号与语言先验和教师特定效应混在一起。关键挑战是估计哪些修正由视觉证据支持，而不仅仅是蒸馏的位置或强度。

Method: VAD在每个学生生成前缀处，使用同一固定教师在相关证据存在与移除两种条件下进行评估，通过中心化对数概率的变化定义ut，作为视觉证据方向的带符号代理。VAD将原始修正投影到该代理上，得到干预对齐分量和代理无法解释的残差，并从前者重构学生锚定的目标。训练时该重构目标作为主要监督信号，特权教师仅提供弱正则化。

Result: 在4B和9B规模下的六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。令牌级和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关视觉修正，并产生更强的目标偏移，尤其在证据反驳错误答案时效果显著。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重构是替代源混合监督的有效方案，能够更精准地将视觉证据信号从混合源中分离出来用于蒸馏。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [103] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon，一种面向智能体视觉推理的多模态大语言模型。通过引入Mode Adaptiveness（模式自适应性）和Tool Effect（工具效应）两个维度重新审视智能体视觉推理，并基于"必要性感知自适应奖励"和"提示引导的能力扩展机制"在强化学习阶段分别提升自适应工具调用能力和最具挑战性问题的工具使用能力，实现了更强的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型存在两个关键问题：(1) Mode Adaptiveness不足——模型无法准确判断何时真正需要工具，导致不必要的计算开销；(2) 工具使用在困难样本上带来的增益在很大程度上被简单样本上引入的额外错误所抵消，即Tool Effect不佳。这促使作者希望设计一个既能自适应调用工具、又能真正带来工具增益的模型。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，在两个维度上进行改进：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward（必要性感知自适应奖励），鼓励模型根据任务必要性自适应地决定是否调用工具；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism（提示引导的能力扩展机制），在强化学习阶段强化模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon取得了强于现有方法的整体性能，同时在Mode Adaptiveness和Tool Effect两个维度上均有显著提升，实现了真正的工具诱导性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过从Mode Adaptiveness和Tool Effect两个维度系统分析现有模型的不足，Beacon证明了在强化学习阶段同时优化自适应工具调用判断和工具使用能力，可以既降低不必要的计算开销，又避免工具使用对简单样本的负面影响，从而获得更高效、更可靠的智能体视觉推理。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [104] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chimera，一种混合视觉扩散骨干网络，通过Kimi Delta Attention（O(N)复杂度）、交错多潜头注意力（MLA）和模态感知短卷积的组合，在无位置嵌入的单一光栅顺序流中处理文本、图像和视频token。配合异构架构的HeteroP缩放方案和Chinchilla风格计算最优定律，训练了11B参数（2B激活）的模型，实现了比全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线高1.7倍（稠密）到7.3倍（完整系统）的计算效率，并支持从5秒训练片段零样本外推到30秒视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，全注意力的二次方计算成本变得不可承受。现有模型在处理长序列时面临计算瓶颈，亟需一种兼具线性复杂度、全局交互能力和局部时空建模能力的高效视觉扩散架构。

Method: Chimera采用三种注意力机制的混合设计：Kimi Delta Attention（KDA）负责O(N)复杂度的长上下文状态追踪，交错的多头潜注意力（MLA）提供直接的全局交互，模态感知短卷积捕捉局部时空上下文。所有token在无位置嵌入的单一光栅流中处理。稀疏MoE层在不增加激活计算的情况下扩展容量。针对异构架构提出HeteroP模块级超参数迁移方案，基于张量的功能扇入和模型深度跨宽度和深度传递超参数，用于拟合计算最优定律（模型规模、训练token数和图像-视频数据比例的Chinchilla风格关系）。

Result: 三项主要实验结果：1）预训练扩散损失显示稠密骨干比匹配的全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线计算效率高1.7倍，完整系统达7.3倍；2）无需长度特化微调，模型可从5秒训练片段零样本外推至30秒视频，最后五秒仅6.5%的FID退化；3）拟合定律表明图像预训练在激活模型规模和训练token数间近乎均分计算预算，而视频预训练在高预算下适度偏向模型规模。

Conclusion: Chimera证明了混合注意力架构配合系统化缩放方案能够大幅提升长上下文视觉扩散模型的计算效率，为设计和扩展高效长上下文扩散架构奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [105] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一种基于"物理语言"（一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示）构建的物理世界模型。PhiZero采用"先推理后渲染"的范式：先用物理语言序列推断未来的世界演化，再将推断结果渲染为视频。在生成和理解基准上的大量实验验证了其建模物理连贯世界演化的能力，并展示了其在真实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理世界模型通常直接在像素空间中预测未来视频，使得潜在的世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中而不明确。受人类能够从视觉经验中抽象出预测结构并用自然语言进行显式推理的启发，本文旨在通过一种紧凑的离散表示（物理语言）来显式地推理物理世界的演化。

Method: PhiZero首先从野外视频中通过自监督学习获得物理语言（一种紧凑离散的世界状态转换表示），然后采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）范式：先推断未来的世界演化（以物理语言序列形式），再将该序列渲染为视频。

Result: 在生成和理解基准上的大量实验表明，PhiZero能够建模物理连贯的世界演化；此外，它在现实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面展现出潜力。

Conclusion: PhiZero通过显式的物理语言推理代替隐式的像素级预测，为物理世界模型提供了一种更可解释、更高效的建模方式，并展示了在多种下游任务上的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [106] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（环境捕捉引擎），一种将真实家庭环境转变为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室的人体中心数据引擎，可同时以桌面尺度和房间尺度记录egocentric/多视角exocentric视频、全身与手部运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频和触觉信号。基于ACE构建的ACE-Data-0数据集包含150小时、1700万帧、200个任务类别、5万名参与者在2个环境中的75,000个交互片段，并引入分层基准从信号到场景组件再到交互进行评测，揭示现有SOTA方法在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度下的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈：模型需要捕捉第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉随时间协同演化的完整感知-行动循环，但现有数据集在视角、模态或空间尺度上碎片化，无法完整观测这一循环。

Method: ACE以两种互补尺度运作：桌面尺度配置解析手-物体操作，房间尺度配置捕捉全身运动、移动和跨家具家庭交互；统一记录多模态同步数据流（egocentric+多视角exocentric视频、全身和关节手部运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频、触觉）。数据集通过目标级而非逐步指令指导参与者，保留自然行为变异；并提出分层基准从信号到场景组件再到交互递进评测。

Result: 构建了ACE-Data-0数据集（150小时、1700万帧、200个任务类别、50名参与者、2个环境、75,000个交互片段）；分层基准评测显示SOTA方法在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度场景下存在实质性性能缺口。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供感知、运动学和接触信号同步对齐的人类示范数据，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统和具身AI提供可扩展的基础，有望缓解具身智能的数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [107] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken是一个可学习的检索目标嵌入，用于从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择查询相关的稀疏视觉token，以解决长视觉上下文中VLM性能退化与GPU显存瓶颈问题，在小型图像QA数据集上训练即能显著提升图像和视频基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉语言模型构成两大挑战：一是随着干扰物数量增加，模型性能显著下降；二是全部token一次性处理在GPU显存限制下计算不可行。现有方法缺乏一种高效且可扩展的方案来筛选与查询相关的视觉信息。

Method: 提出ReToken，一个单一的可学习嵌入，被训练为显式的检索目标，用于从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择稀疏的查询相关视觉token。该方法仅需在小型图像QA数据集上训练，借助轻量化设计，训练和长视频推理均可在一张H100上完成。

Result: 在Visual Haystacks基准上，ReToken使Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4个百分点、InternVL3.5提升12.4个百分点（相对提升超过20%）；在LVBench长视频基准上实现零样本迁移，Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0个百分点增益。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量的可学习检索嵌入机制，在极小的训练代价下有效解决了长视觉上下文下的性能退化与显存约束问题，并在图像与视频任务上展现了良好的跨模态泛化能力。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多头注意力残差（MHAR），通过将路由查询重塑为H个子空间头，每个头对深度历史使用独立的softmax，使残差流的读取从单一分布变为块对角结构。在100M/350M/1B规模上验证损失均优于标准Transformer，最佳结果优于其他四种方法，增益随规模增大而增加，并在8B规模上通过零参数转换实现数学和推理基准的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的注意力残差机制使用单一查询（单一softmax分布）在整条宽度上读取深度历史，但不同特征子空间对于"应读取哪些层"的偏好往往不同，且这种分歧随模型宽度增大而加剧。这种被迫的折中（forced compromise）会损害信息路由效率，需要一种能让不同子空间独立选择深度读取位置的方法。

Method: 将注意力残差中的路由查询重构为H个子空间头（每个头有独立的softmax），组成块对角读取结构；该重塑零参数增加、计算量可忽略；H=1时精确退化为原始注意力残差。训练在去重、质量过滤、STEM与代码密集的Nemotron退火语料上进行。同时设计了融合Triton路由内核提升吞吐，以及基于delta注意力残差的保恒等转换以支持8B中期训练。

Result: 在100M、350M、1B规模上验证损失分别改善-0.061、-0.149、-0.140；四种方法中在所有设置下均取得最佳，增益随规模增大而增强。H存在U型最优（平坦最优在H=4或8），过拆分（H=16）会回吐部分增益。Triton内核使训练吞吐从0.2-0.5x提升至0.55-0.88x基线，峰值内存接近基线。8B中期训练在GSM8K上+3.2、GPQA上+3.1。直接探针实验证实学习到的子空间分歧是增益的驱动力。

Conclusion: MHAR证明多头化深度读取是Transformer架构改进中的有效设计轴，头数H是真实的设计维度而非自由旋钮；子空间分歧是深度路由效率损失的根源，MHAR通过解耦该冲突带来一致且可规模化的收益，同时保持计算开销和内存占用近乎不变。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [109] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelGenBench，一个用于系统性评估LLM和智能体生成Triton内核的统一基准，涵盖多算子来源（210个算子）和异构硬件平台（6种芯片），并通过大规模评估（消耗超150亿token）揭示：智能体方法优于纯LLM采样，跨平台性能退化严重，且自主内核生成成本极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和智能体框架为自动内核生成带来新机遇，但缺乏一个统一的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在不同算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现，难以系统衡量和比较各类方法的实际能力。

Method: 构建KernelGenBench统一基准，包含两个子基准：(1) KernelGenBench-MS（多源），评估来自三种来源的210个算子；(2) KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片），使用110个算子子集衡量跨6种异构硬件平台的性能可移植性。通过大规模评估（消耗超150亿token）系统比较纯LLM采样和智能体方法。

Result: (1) 智能体方法一致优于纯LLM采样方法，cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2) 不同硬件平台间生成性能差异显著，内核专用智能体也面临严重跨平台退化（如AutoKernel从NVIDIA上的87%降至平台E上的25%）；(3) 自主内核生成成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达519万），比简单LLM采样高数个数量级。

Conclusion: KernelGenBench为LLM/智能体生成内核提供了首个统一评估框架，揭示了当前方法的性能瓶颈（跨平台可移植性差）和成本问题（token消耗巨大），为后续内核生成研究提供了基准和方向指引。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [110] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源Python库，提供统一API用于加载、协调和增强放射学数据集，主要面向胸部X光片并支持CT和MRI。它标准化了24个公共数据集的元数据，封装MONAI数据集，通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射报告文本，并引入AI-agent技能辅助新数据集的集成流程。通过RadHarmony-ViT基线模型展示了其实用性，代码和预训练权重已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射图像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录布局、标签模式和注释类型的异构数据集，这一过程缺乏统一的工具支持，导致数据集成成本高、代码复用性差。

Method: RadHarmony提供：1) 统一API实现放射学数据集的加载、协调和增强；2) 将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式；3) 封装MONAI的map-style数据集以支持深学习就绪的样本交付和可选磁盘缓存；4) 通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射报告文本；5) 提供交互式可视化工具用于数据集探索和验证；6) 引入AI-agent技能引导从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试的完整集成工作流。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT（一个参考视觉Transformer基线模型）证明了库的实用性，该模型无需数据集特定代码即可组合三个异构胸部X光数据集。代码和预训练模型权重已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: RadHarmony通过统一的数据处理接口和AI辅助集成工具，显著降低了放射学多数据集整合的复杂性和技术门槛，为遥感影像深度学习研究提供了标准化、可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [111] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将Gemma-3-1B-IT分别针对CARES危害等级分类和WildJailbreak对抗性拒答两个安全目标进行微调，再用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种合并方法融合模型，发现攻击拒答能力的迁移显著优于分类精度（拒答率保留81-85%，而分类精度最多仅12.9%）。研究表明这种不对称并非源于任务向量方向冲突（余弦相似度仅0.011），而是因为拒答微调产生更大的逐层任务向量幅度，使得对幅度敏感的合并方法偏向拒答更新，导致标准模型合并将安全识别退化为广泛拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常用于无需额外训练即可组合多个微调模型的能力，但标准合并方法能否同时保留多个安全相关行为尚不清楚。本文旨在通过受控案例研究揭示多安全目标下模型合并的行为表现及其背后的权重空间机制。

Method: 使用Gemma-3-1B-IT模型，分别针对CARES危害级别分类和WildJailbreak对抗性拒答两个补充性安全目标进行微调；采用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种合并方法融合两个微调模型；在分类精度、攻击抵抗力和良性合规三个维度上评估合并模型；通过任务向量余弦相似度和逐层任务向量幅度等权重空间测量分析不对称性的原因。

Result: 四种合并方法下攻击拒答率保留81-85%，而CARES分类精度最多仅12.9%，显示攻击拒答的迁移显著强于分类精度。任务向量余弦相似度仅为0.011（几乎正交），排除了方向冲突的解释；拒答微调产生更大的逐层任务向量幅度，导致幅度敏感的合并方法系统性偏向拒答更新。

Conclusion: 当安全相关的任务向量在幅度上存在显著差异时，标准模型合并会导致安全识别能力崩溃，退化为广泛的拒答行为。这表明幅度不对称是模型合并中安全行为失衡的关键因素，为安全敏感的模型合并实践提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [112] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过Jensen-Shannon散度监控通用模型与领域专家模型在每个token上的分布分歧，在检测到推理风险时动态将控制权从专家路由到通用模型，实现两种模型能力的动态融合，在GPQA、ChemBench等科学基准上超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用推理上表现出色但缺乏专业科学领域知识；领域专家模型拥有专业知识却存在推理能力减弱和鲁棒性下降等专化副作用。现有方法无法同时兼顾专业知识和通用推理能力，需要一种无需训练的框架来实现两者能力的融合。

Method: 借鉴投机解码的"草稿-验证"框架，将其重构为自适应路由机制。核心是使用Jensen-Shannon散度在每个token位置监控专家模型与通用模型之间的分布分歧。当专家模型的输出与通用模型产生显著分歧时，判定为潜在推理风险，立即将控制权路由给通用模型，从而在保留领域专业知识的同时动态注入通用推理能力，实现推理时的策略组合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列多种模型族上，使用GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等科学基准进行评估，Divergence Decoding同时优于领域专用模型和通用模型，且超越了大多数单一模型基线。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的推理时协作范式，能够通过自适应推理时协作有效融合不同LLM的能力，为缓解通用模型与领域专家模型之间的能力权衡问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [113] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，包含PIE-Abducer（增量直接推导溯因）和PIE-APT（时序知识图谱溯因规划）两个集成模块，在表达性描述逻辑上原生操作，解决开放世界环境中动态知识图谱上的推理与规划问题。通过将状态转换建模为对演绎封闭DL理论的非单调更新，并将增量推理器视为黑盒、以OWL原生表示动作，保持了逻辑可判定性。PIE-Abducer通过向一致分支注入目标否定并提取直接反驳结果中的缺失前提，避免最小命中集枚举；PIE-APT采用递归的生成-测试架构，将后向链式A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交织至有界因果深度，再经前向时序投影严格验证。在四个OWL基准上评估，展示了相较于经典规划器的定性优势，并在溯因丰富过程中定量优于MHS基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱上的推理与规划在开放世界环境下面临重大挑战：现有动作形式化常存在可判定性问题与分支问题（Ramification Problem），而通过结构溯因管理不完备知识需要大规模的组合搜索。亟需一个既能保持逻辑可判定性、又能高效处理不完备信息的统一框架。

Method: 提出两个集成模块：(1) PIE-Abducer采用直接推导溯因，通过向一致分支注入目标否定并提取直接反驳结果中的缺失前提，规避传统最小命中集枚举的组合穷举；(2) PIE-APT采用递归生成-测试架构，将后向链式A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交织至有界因果深度，再用前向时序投影进行严格验证。状态转换建模为对演绎封闭描述逻辑理论的非单调更新，动作原生表示在OWL中，无需外部模态算子。

Result: 在四个OWL基准上评估，这些基准强调经典规划中缺失的语义能力：参数化目标与见证搜索、搜索过程中的DL蕴涵、开放世界假设注入以及对抗性矛盾猎取。实验结果显示该方法在定性上优于经典规划器，且直接推导方法在溯因丰富过程中定量优于忠实于MHS的基线。

Conclusion: 通过将增量推理器视为黑盒并以OWL原生表示动作，该方法在保持逻辑可判定性的同时，有效解决了动态知识图谱上的开放世界规划问题，其直接推导溯因方法在性能上优于传统的最小命中集枚举基线，为时序知识图谱溯因规划提供了可行且高效的新方案。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [114] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAUSE，一个面向个人AI助手的有状态、服务集成环境基准测试，通过模拟真实用户和约束感知的多轮交互来评估助手能力，实验表明即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的任务上也难以达到70%的完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往将服务上下文碎片化或抽象掉用户状态，无法在真实服务场景中评估以用户为中心的个人助手行为。真实部署中的助手需要在统一服务环境中推理持久用户状态、尊重用户特定配置和权限，并在多服务上维持长时间跨度的约束感知交互，但这些核心挑战缺乏充分的评测手段。

Method: PAUSE通过以下方式构建：1) 在状态化、服务集成环境中要求智能体协调跨异构用户资源的操作，同时保持与环境状态的一致性以及多轮交互中的授权约束；2) 引入显式用户-智能体交互，通过现实用户模拟实现超越静态工具执行的评估；3) 采用多体制评估框架，对开放式服务管理任务使用语义和轨迹级行为指标，对约束密集型任务采用确定性的基于状态的验证；4) 提出用户为中心的综合合成流水线，实现可扩展的服务环境、用户配置和可靠标注任务生成。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景下任务完成率仍低于70%，并暴露出一致且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAUSE填补了个人AI助手在真实服务场景评估中的空白，提供了可扩展的基准构建方案和可复现的评估框架，为未来面向用户中心的个人助手研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [115] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略来生成诊断任务、识别盲点并整理为可复用的纠错技能，从而在不更新执行器权重、不依赖人工标注的情况下实现Agent自我进化。在AppWorld和BFCLv3上平均提升执行器性能44.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent自我进化研究主要关注"如何行动"的学习，而忽视了"发现自身未知领域"这一同等重要的能力。已有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，只关注如何修复已识别的失败。本文提出盲点诊断本身也应作为可学习的能力，而非依赖执行器自适应或人工监督。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，使其具备三种能力：(1)生成诊断任务以探测执行器盲点；(2)识别反复出现的失败模式；(3)将失败模式整理为可复用的纠错技能。实验设计上严格排除执行器自适应和人工监督两种外部进步来源，确保所有性能提升均源自诊断能力的改进。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准上，SkillMentor平均提升执行器性能44.2%，且不更新执行器权重、不依赖人工标注数据。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是可以学习的独立能力，Agent可以通过学习诊断自身未知领域来实现自我进化，无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工整理的数据。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型(DLM)与自回归模型在噪声和对抗攻击下的鲁棒性与校准性，发现DLM虽天然抵抗梯度攻击，但无噪声防御保障，且存在系统性过度自信；机制探针显示模型能完美编码输入损坏，脆弱性源于解码器路由失败，提示鲁棒性必须内建到迭代解码循环中而非表面修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归生成的有力替代方案已在双向上下文和迭代精炼方面展现潜力，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未被充分探索，这直接影响其实际部署安全性。

Method: 采用参数匹配的成对模型(LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B, Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B),在32种自然扰动条件下进行鲁棒性评估，并结合对抗梯度探针和机制性隐状态分析，通过配对设计隔离架构固有属性与权重依赖行为。

Result: 发现DLM的鲁棒性画像具有复杂性：高度随机的损失景观天然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但无法防御自然噪声；DLM表现出系统性过度自信；机制探针揭示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全源于解码器路由失败，表面提示修补无法改善。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过外部补丁获得，必须从根本上整合进迭代解码循环设计中，意味着需要内建式的鲁棒性机制而非事后修补。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [117] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMER框架，通过构建动态的行动中心图（Action-Centric Graph）将推理扩展与情景记忆相结合，使用双流时序差分学习评估行动节点的正向（建议）与负向（规避）价值，从而在推理阶段双向优化决策，显著提升智能体在推断时扩展的效率，在多个基准上成功率提升20.81%、进展率提升6.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）方法虽能增强LLM的复杂推理能力，但对智能体而言存在严重的效率问题：以无状态方式运行，导致搜索过程重复冗余。已有的记忆机制高度依赖LLM自身的推理能力，计算成本高昂。因此需要一种将推理扩展与情景记忆有效结合、且不依赖LLM推理的轻量级记忆机制。

Method: GAMER框架将历史推理过程建模为动态的Action-Centric Graph（行动中心图），将记忆机制与LLM解耦以减少token/成本消耗。通过双流时序差分（Temporal Difference）学习机制，分别估计行动节点的正向价值（建议执行）和负向价值（高风险规避），从历史成功与失败中提取知识。推理阶段利用学习到的价值函数进行双向优化决策，并在图上执行高效搜索以提升推理扩展的效率。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，GAMER相比vanilla基线在成功率上提升20.81%、进展率上提升6.17%，同时在提供更少记忆上下文的情况下优于记忆机制基线。

Conclusion: GAMER有效弥合了推理扩展与情景记忆之间的差距，通过行动中心图和双流价值学习，实现了更高效、更经济的智能体推理扩展，证明解耦记忆机制与结构化的经验提取可以显著提升智能体的决策性能。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [118] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文采用心理测量学方法（二参数逻辑斯蒂IRT模型）对HLE基准的428道文本型单选题进行评估，发现HLE测量的是单一通用推理因子（McDonald's ω_h = 0.998），领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目反应方差，领域子分数与总分近乎冗余（r ≥ 0.81）。同时，测试信息函数显示测量精度集中于中等能力水平，在前沿模型所处的高能力区域（θ > 0）测量精度急剧下降，说明该基准无法有效区分最强模型之间的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLE被广泛用于评估前沿大语言模型，其八个学科领域类别的子分数常被解读为不同能力的证据，但尚无研究验证这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，也没有研究评估该基准在相似能力模型间的区分效能。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对29个大语言模型在HLE文本单选的428个项目上进行评估，采用二参数逻辑斯蒂（2PL）IRT模型进行拟合，并通过多项指标评估基准的维度和测量精度分布，包括McDonald's ω_h、领域标签对项目反应方差的解释比例、域内与域间残差相关性比较（Cohen's d）、领域特定能力估计与总分之间的相关性，以及测试信息函数分析。

Result: 收敛性证据表明HLE测量的是单一通用推理因子：ω_h = 0.998；领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目反应方差；域内与域间残差相关性几乎相同（Cohen's d = 0.016）；领域特定能力估计与总分相关性 ≥ 0.81。测试信息函数分析显示测量精度集中于中等能力水平，在θ > 0（前沿模型所在区域）处急剧下降。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不构成独立能力维度的有效解释依据，该基准在区分最强模型间的能力差异方面存在局限。建议研究者不应将HLE的领域子分数解读为不同能力的证据，并需谨慎使用HLE评估前沿模型的相对性能。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [119] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图神经网络建模历史轨迹为概率图，在执行前诊断潜在动作错误，为LLM智能体提供早期预警促使其自我纠正，从而解决长时程交互任务中的累积误差问题，在四个基准上平均提升14.69%的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程交互任务中常因累积误差而失败——单个次优动作可能使整个轨迹偏离，导致智能体在有限的步数预算内陷入低效或不可恢复的路径。传统的微调方案成本高昂，需要一种无需昂贵微调的轻量级纠错机制。

Method: 核心组件为Graph Debugger：将历史轨迹建模为概率图，使用图神经网络识别频繁导致失败的顺序动作模式。该方法作为主动式诊断沙箱，在动作执行前提供早期错误预警，促使智能体自我纠正，实现对潜在有缺陷动作的预判。

Result: 在四个基准上使用三种LLM智能体进行广泛实验，平均通过率提升14.69%。

Conclusion: 预动作错误诊断能够有效防止代价高昂的失误，显著增强LLM智能体在长时程交互任务中的完成能力，为无需微调的性能提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully.
